# Source-term order ledger for Steps 3–5

This ledger locates terms of `GB-FINAL-DENSITY` using the already existing assembly labels. It records their **new background expansion**, without rechecking or changing the input action. The coefficient includes the accepted Weyl/LOG multipliers; the exterior coupling is $`\lambda`$. The two-derivative terms are individually grouped and evaluated in Step 3, equations (3.17)–(3.18), and Step 5, equations (5.1)–(5.2).

For zero rows, the displayed product evaluation is the actual reason for both coefficients: two odd factors or two zero-background bosonic factors give $`(tX_1)(tY_1)A_0=t^2A_0X_1Y_1`$, so $`[\cdot]_0=[\cdot]_1=0`$. Nonzero scalar entries point to their **fully evaluated** $`t^0,t^1`$ rows in Steps 4 and 5. The two epsilon contractions are evaluated explicitly in (3.16). There is no discarded total derivative hidden in a zero row. The only surviving first-order boundary currents are (5.7).
